### 3.3 Scenario harness seams

The scenario harness (section 3.4; the soak and fresh-session batches are section 3.5) drives real fights in Play mode. It relies on:

- `_Core/ScenarioHooks.cs`: inert static hooks (dice filter, attack, AoO, maneuver, threatened-cast, cast begun (NPC path and, since batch 11 step 3, the PC pipelines), cast stage, move, condition, combat-log and phase events, scripted AI turns, forced initiative head, input suppression). Raise sites and what each one covers are listed in [architecture/combat-and-grid.md](architecture/combat-and-grid.md#scenario-hooks-test-instrumentation). Install handlers through `ScenarioHooks.Safe` and `ScenarioHooks.SafeTurn` and fail the run when `ScenarioHooks.HookErrors` is not empty. Call `ScenarioHooks.ClearAll()` when a run ends. Leftovers are also cleared: Enter Play Mode Options are off, so statics survive leaving Play mode, and `Tests/Scenarios/ScenarioSessionGuard.cs` (editor only, `[InitializeOnLoad]`) restores fast mode and clears the hooks on ExitingPlayMode and EnteredEditMode; `StaticSuiteRunner.Run` does the same before a pass and adds a report warning when anything was still set.
- `_Core/GameManager.ScenarioHarness.cs` (editor only, `internal`): `Harness_IsReady`, `Harness_SetupPartyFromCreation` (returns a reason when level-ups queued by creation were left unapplied), `Harness_SetupPartySlot` (set BAB through `BaseAttackBonusOverride`, CHR-068), `Harness_DeactivatePartySlot`, `Harness_SpawnEnemies` (the squares given, through the game's spawn placement; a negative square gets the default layout; the runner then places exact actors with `MoveToCell`), `Harness_StartCombat`, `Harness_HaltCombat` (stops GameManager and TurnService coroutines, so run the harness on its own MonoBehaviour), `Harness_ResetWorld` (runs every step even if one throws and returns the failed steps; summons are destroyed at the end of the frame), `Harness_RestorePartyAfterCombat`, `Harness_DumpState`, `Harness_PendingPrompt` (the open AoO confirmation, ranged retarget, full-attack 5-ft choice or special-style submenu, else null), `Harness_CancelToActionChoices`, `Harness_OpenSpecialAttackMenu` (presses Special Attack and reports whether the menu opened; a selecting flag already set before the press is reported, never cleared), `Harness_PressSpecialAttackButton` (clicks the open menu's button for a maneuver type, refused when that button is hidden or disabled), `Harness_SelectSpecialAttack`, `Harness_SelectSpell`, `Harness_SetPendingCast`, `Harness_GetPendingSpell` and `Harness_GetPendingMetamagic` (set and read the pending cast's spell and metamagic with no other effect; they replace reflection on `_pendingSpell` and `_pendingMetamagic`, TST-006, which are read-only views of the current cast's request since batch 11 step 29; with no cast open a spell opens a scratch Ui cast and null ends it), `Harness_OpenCastCount`, `Harness_CurrentCastRequest` and `Harness_EndAllCasts` (the per-cast context stack, batch 11 step 4), `Harness_CancelSelection` (right-click or Escape, `HandleInputCancelRequested`: the player backs out of a target selection or preview; `rules/spell-save-dc`'s handler Heighten check uses them), `Harness_ApplySpellBuff` (a buff spell's effect through `ApplySpellBuff`, the effect step of a landed cast, without slot or action; pass a clone), `Harness_AnswerTouchSpellPromptDischargeLater` and `Harness_ChooseFirstSelectionOption` (the Discharge Later button and the open selection list's first option, for the `CastHeld` and `CastChoosing` steps; batch 11 step 3, 2026-10-15, with the read accessor `CombatUI.Harness_FindFirstSelectionOption`), `Harness_SpawnSummonedCreature` and `Harness_DespawnSummon` (the game's summon spawn `SpawnSummonedCreature` for a caster, cell and summon option, without initiative or the active summon list, and its immediate removal from the grid, the summon trackers, initiative, `NPCs` and the AI behaviour list), `Harness_PendingAoO`, `Harness_AnswerAoO`, `Harness_PendingCounterTripDefender` and `Harness_AnswerCounterTrip` (the counter-trip prompt, CMB-079; `Harness_HaltCombat` and the combat reset drop an unanswered one). `Harness_ResetWorld` also releases every party, pool and summon grapple before the summons are destroyed: grapple links are static and outlive a halted fight (CMB-038), and without that a reused slot started the next job still grappling. Two editor-only read accessors back this: `CombatUI.Harness_IsSpecialAttackMenuOpen`, `Harness_FindSpecialAttackButton`, `Harness_OpenSpecialStyleMenuName`, `Harness_FindSpecialStyleOption` and `Harness_FindSpecialStyleCancel` (the open selection submenu, its option by label and its Cancel button; CMB-127, CMB-124), and `CharacterController.Harness_GrappleLinkHolders` (the static link table, read without ending any link). Both party setups reset a slot that held a character through `GameManager.ResetPCSlotForNewCharacter` (the same `ResetCharacterSlotForSpawn` the enemy spawns use, CRE-046) before `Init`: `Harness_SetupPartySlot` directly and `Harness_SetupPartyFromCreation` through `SetupCreatedCharacters`. So a party character of one job does not inherit the previous job's Power Attack, Fighting Defensively, Rapid Shot, readied counterspell, attack pool, AI maneuver memory, tags, trackers, mount, feint windows, turn state, spell effects or spellcasting component, and casters get a fresh `SpellcastingComponent`. The harness does not reset enemy pool slots itself: `Harness_SpawnEnemies` goes through `SetupEnemyEncounter` and `InitializeNPCFromDefinition`, which first calls `GameManager.ResetCharacterSlotForSpawn` (CRE-046, fixed 2026-10-08), so every spawned slot starts as a fresh controller: no trait, component, spell effect, grapple or mount link, tag, AI memory or profile of its earlier creature (the old profile is destroyed only when the GameManager created it, never one the harness assigned), and no other character's feint window, readied counterspell, remembered square, concentration or aura save immunity tied to it (the list is in [architecture/characters-and-creatures.md](architecture/characters-and-creatures.md#from-definition-to-live-combatant)). A slot left inactive by the reset keeps its last creature until it is spawned again, which the clean check does not look at. Since the fix a soak seed gives the same trace hash in a batch session as alone in a fresh session (seeds 21-40 checked, section 3.5). Being `internal`, they are reachable from the harness in Assembly-CSharp but not from an MCP `CommandScript`.
- `CharacterCreationUI.CompleteWithParty(party, flowSource)`: the end of Play Now! and Quick Start, callable with any non-empty party (returns false and changes nothing for a null or empty one).
- `Tests.Scenarios.ScenarioFastMode` (editor only, public): `Apply("fast")` sets timeScale 30, captureFramerate 30, maximumDeltaTime 1, uncapped frame rate, run in background, logs below Warning filtered and dice logging off; `Apply("watch")` is real time with every other saved setting back (logs and dice logging visible again); any other mode is refused. `Restore()` puts the saved settings back. QualitySettings (vSync) are never written, so nothing can leak into ProjectSettings. `StartProbe()` and a later `ProbeResult()` measure waits under fast mode; a mode the harness had already applied stays applied afterwards.
- **Silent stalls.** Console Error Pause pauses Play on any `LogError` or exception: when polling a run, check `EditorApplication.isPaused` and unpause (or turn Error Pause off before a run). The other stall is editor throttling when Unity is not focused (see the probe below). A third, seen on 2026-10-09, is a stall on entering Play mode, focused or not (TST-036; the batch 7 result below and section 3.5), probably from domain reloads that grow slower over a long editor session (TST-037).

**Fast-mode probe of 2026-10-07** (Play mode, editor focused, MainScene at character creation): every `WaitForSeconds(0.8f)` and every `WaitForSeconds(0.08f)` took exactly 1 frame (20 of each), and each frame advanced scaled time by 1.0 s (captureFramerate 30 x timeScale 30; maximumDeltaTime 1 did not clamp it lower). The 40 waits took 41 frames and 0.073 s of real time (about 1.5-1.8 ms per frame); the game ran at about 650 frames per second between MCP calls. Every scaled wait in game code therefore costs one frame, and anything paced by `Time.deltaTime` jumps 1 s per frame (poison secondaries, CMB-007). Settings were restored afterwards (timeScale 1, captureFramerate 0, maximumDeltaTime 0.333). That probe also set vSync, which fast mode no longer touches. A second probe of 2026-10-07 (after the seams review, the probe now counts frames with and without editor focus) gave the same numbers (41 frames, every wait 1 frame, 0.07-0.14 s real time), with fast mode applied by the probe and again with fast mode already applied by the caller (it stayed applied afterwards). Unity reported the editor focused on all 42 frames (`InternalEditorUtility.isApplicationActive`). The batch 7 full regression of 2026-10-09 measured the unfocused case: every job of rules run 20261009-081350 (545 jobs), smoke run 20261009-082000 and soak run 20261009-082525 counted all its frames as unfocused (`editorFocusedFrames` 0) and, once the session had started, ran at normal speed (rules and smoke jobs at most 7-10 s, soak jobs at least 35 frames per second). Entering Play mode is another matter: the first job of the rules session and of every soak session took 84 to 108 s (10 to 667 frames, about 0.1 to 6 frames per second), whether the editor counted as unfocused (runs -081350 and -082525) or focused (soak runs -083038, -084007, -084521 and -085027), while the smoke session did not stall (first job 7.7 s, 15 frames). The `EditorApplication.QueuePlayerLoopUpdate()` call in `ScenarioBatchDriver.WaitAndStart` did not get Play mode past that stall; see TST-036 and the procedure in section 3.5. In the 2026-10-07 probe session a `Debug.LogException` did not pause Play (Error Pause was off).

### 3.4 Scenario harness

`Assets/Scripts/Tests/Scenarios/` (all `#if UNITY_EDITOR`, namespace `Tests.Scenarios`; public so an MCP `CommandScript` can call it) plays real fights: the real `StartCombat`, turn order, AI and rules code, with the actors on exact squares, seeded dice, optional forced dice and a typed trace. Built 2026-10-07 on the seams of section 3.3.

**Files.** `ScenarioModel.cs` (enums, `ActorSource`, `ActorSpec`, `DiceForce`, `Waiver`, `Expectation`, `ScenarioDef`, the `Scenario.Define(...)` builder and `ScenarioCatalog`), `ScenarioRunner.cs` (the job loop on its own DontDestroyOnLoad `ScenarioRunner` object, never on GameManager, CORE-013), `ScenarioTrace.cs` (events, JSON writer, hash), `ScenarioChecks.cs` (invariants, combat-end detection, watchdog), `ScenarioExpect.cs` (`TraceView`, `Expect`, verdict rules), `ScenarioSteps.cs` (typed steps, the AI-path executor and the Ui driver), `KnownIssueWaivers.cs`, `AiProfileForClass.cs`, `ScenarioHarness.cs` (the public API), `ScenarioTraceDiff.cs` (the first divergence of two runs of a job, section 3.5), `ScenarioBatchDriver.cs` (fresh-session batches, section 3.5), `SoakStats.cs` (soak statistics, section 3.5), `Catalog/*.cs` (scenario definitions: any `public static IEnumerable<ScenarioDef>` method marked `[ScenarioSource]` in a `Tests.Scenarios*` namespace is picked up). Catalog today: `smoke/boot`, `smoke/two-goblins-ai`, `smoke/soak-mini`, 156 `rules/*` scenarios in `Catalog/RulesScenarios.cs` (`RulesScenarios.Count`, corrected on 2026-10-08 from a stale 74 and on 2026-10-09 from a stale 113 when `rules/bonus-stacking` was added, then 116 with `rules/prayer-cast`, 118 since 2026-10-14 with `rules/improved-grab-mid-sequence` and `rules/grapple-rake-ui`, 123 later that day with the five grapple review scenarios (the constant was left at 118 then), and 131 with the eight scenarios of the 2026-10-11 maneuver rulings, CMB-134, CMB-141, CMB-150 and CMB-154, counted by `ScenarioHarness.List("rules/*")` on 2026-10-14, then 135 the same day with `rules/combat-end-tie-victory`, `-tie-defeat` and `-charmed-victory` (CORE-038; `List` counted 132 before them), then 136 with `rules/flat-footed-start` (CMB-028), and 137, 138 and 139 the same day with `rules/template-applied-once` (CRE-001), `rules/summon-creature-entries` (CRE-003) and `rules/monster-alignment-data` (CRE-056; the constant, left at 136, set to 139 and checked against `RulesScenarios.All()`), then 140 with `rules/npc-cast-spontaneous` (SPL-123), 142 with `rules/dragon-ai-breath-young` and `-juvenile` (AI-037), 144 with `rules/movement-aoo-withdraw-far` and `-ui` (GRID-001), 146 with `rules/movement-through-ally` and `-ui` (the GRID-001 review), 149 by the end of 2026-10-14 (the XP and grapple scenarios) and 151 with `rules/combat-end-charmed-defeat` and `rules/combat-end-tie-charmed-survivor` (CORE-038), 152 with `rules/combat-end-domination-cleared-by-rest` (the CORE-038 review), 154 with `rules/movement-aoo-withdraw-large` and `-ui` (GRID-021), 157 with `rules/zero-level-spells-per-day`, `rules/zero-level-spells-ai` and `rules/zero-level-spells-aoo-cancel` (SPL-138); listed under "Rules scenarios" below), 23 `rules/cast-*` scenarios in `Catalog/CastPipelineScenarios.cs` (batch 11 step 3, 2026-10-15; listed under "Cast pipeline pins" below) and `soak/dmg-random-encounters` in `Catalog/SoakScenarios.cs` (section 3.5), 259 in all on 2026-10-16 after batch 11 step 21 (255 `rules/*`, the 3 smoke scenarios and the soak; 209 on 2026-10-15, 160 on 2026-10-14): a shorter `List` means a definition did not load. Wrap each `yield return` of a source in `ScenarioCatalog.Safe(name, () => ...)`: a definition that fails validation is then recorded in `loadErrors` and the rest of the source still loads.

**Actors and control.** An actor is `ActorSource.QuickStart(class)` (a class Quick Start character, through `Harness_SetupPartyFromCreation`), `ActorSource.Stats(() => stats)` (an exact `CharacterStats` in a party slot; set BAB through `BaseAttackBonusOverride`, CHR-068) or an NPC id (a pool slot, through `Harness_SpawnEnemies`). At most 4 party-slot actors (QuickStart first, then Stats, in definition order) and 40 NPCs (`ScenarioLimits.MaxNpcActors`; the game's enemy pool grows past its 15 starting slots, ENC-001); positions are validated against the 20x20 grid and placed exactly with `MoveToCell`. An NPC added with `.NpcPlacedByGame(key, id)` (`ActorSpec.GamePlaced`) has no position: the harness passes (-1,-1), the game's default layout places it as it places a random encounter (`EncounterSpawnPlacement`), and the runner leaves it there (the actor event's `pos` records the square); an exact actor whose square it took fails setup. A definition can also generate actors per job with `.GenerateActors(ctx => ...)`: it runs right after the RNG is seeded with the job seed (so a seed always gives the same actors), its actors join a per-job copy of the definition that is validated again, its `Info` goes to the `generated` trace event and the job result, and its `Cleanup` runs after the job's reset. `ActorSource.Npc(id, label)` shows `label` in the trace instead of an id made per job. Control per actor:

| Control | Turn path | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Ai` | `AIService.ExecuteNPCTurn` | A party-slot actor is made non-controllable and gets a class profile (`AiProfileForClass`: Cleric and Druid Healer; Wizard and Sorcerer Evoker with a damage spell, else Spellcaster; Ranger with a ranged weapon Ranged; else Humanoid). It then takes the NPC path, not the PC path (see the limits below). |
| `Scripted` | `ExecuteNPCTurn` up to the compulsion gates, then the actor's typed steps for the round (`.Turn(key, round, steps...)`, round 0 = every round without its own), else its coroutine (`ScenarioDef.Scripts`), else an empty turn, or the AI for an actor listed in `.AiWhenUnscripted(keys)` (traced as controller `ai`) | Frightened, Panicked and Turned actors are handed back to the AI. The automatic stand-up from prone is skipped, so a script stands up explicitly. |
| `Idle` | as `Scripted`, with an empty turn | |
| `Ui` | the PC turn (`StartPCTurn`) | Party slots only. The actor's typed steps for the round run through the PC button and cell-click callbacks; when they run out (at once when there are none) the harness presses End Turn (`OnEndTurnButtonPressed`) and traces a `step` event. |

**Scripted steps** (`ScenarioSteps.cs`; the builder takes `.Turn(actor, round, params Step[])`, `.Idle(keys)` and `.AiWhenUnscripted(keys)`; the definition is validated, so a step its actor's path cannot run is a build error). Every step ends as a `step` trace event (`actor`, `path` ai or ui, index, label, `status`, `note`): `done`; `refused` (nothing changed: no game event, and the actor's square, action economy and conditions are the same; a refused AI-path `Maneuver` or `Cast` notes the standard and full-round action, whether another attack step can be committed, `CanPerformSpecialAttack` and Grappled, because fast mode filters the game's own reason); `skipped` (an `AnswerAoO` or `AnswerImprovedGrab` with no prompt open, or either on the AI path); `dropped` (the actor went down, combat ended, the turn passed, or an `EndTurn` step ran); `error` (an AI-path executor threw: `ScenarioHooks.SafeEnumerate` recorded it in `HookErrors`, which fails the job, and the script went on).

| Step | AI path (Scripted actor) | Ui path (Ui actor) |
|---|---|---|
| `Move(x,y)` | `MoveCharacterAlongComputedPathForAI`, then the move action is spent (or the standard converted) as AIService does at its own call sites; refused after a 5-ft step or with no move action | Move button, then the square |
| `Withdraw(x,y)` | `ExecuteWithdrawMovementForAI` (needs the full round) | Withdraw button, then the square |
| `FiveFootStep(x,y)` | `TryTakeFiveFootStepForAI` | 5-Foot Step button, then the square |
| `StandUp()` | `TryStandUpFromProneForAI` | Stand Up button (the AoO confirmation follows) |
| `Crawl(x,y)`, `DropProne()` | not allowed (no NPC executor) | Crawl button and square; Drop Prone button |
| `Attack(t)`, `AttackAgain(t)` | `NPCPerformAttackForAI`: the creature's whole attack sequence, so a full attack while the full round is free and one attack after a move | one click of the Attack button and the target square (`AttackAgain` is the next click of the iterative sequence) |
| `Flurry(t)` | `CharacterController.TryBeginFlurryOfBlows` (refused with the rules' reason: not a monk, armor, shield, load, weapon, no full round), then `NPCPerformAttackForAI`, which steps the flurry (added 2026-10-14, CHR-003). A plain `Attack` step lets the AI choose the flurry itself (`AIProfile.ShouldUseFlurryOfBlows`) | the Flurry of Blows button (declares the flurry and starts the progressive attack sequence), then the target: the first flurry step; continue with `AttackAgain` or a `Maneuver` step, as in any PC attack sequence |
| `Smite(t)` | `SmiteRules.TryDeclare` (refused with the rule's reason: no smite, already declared, no use left; it declares whatever the target's alignment, so a wasted smite can be scripted), then `NPCPerformAttackForAI` (added 2026-10-14, CHR-020). A plain `Attack` step lets the AI choose the smite itself (`AIProfile.ShouldDeclareSmite`) | the Smite button (it declares; refused when nothing was declared), then the Attack button and the target: the first attack of the sequence |
| `LayOnHands(t)` | `LayOnHandsRules.CanUse`, then `GameManager.TryLayOnHandsForAI` (added 2026-10-14, CHR-020) | the Lay on Hands button, then the target's square (the actor's own square for itself) |
| `WildShape(formId)` | `WildShapeRules.CanAssume` (refused with the rule's reason: no wild shape, already in a form, no use left, standard action used, grappling), then `GameManager.TryWildShapeForAI` into the bestiary animal `formId` (added 2026-10-14, CHR-020) | the Wild Shape button, then the form's option in the form list (`Harness_ChooseWildShapeForm`, which clicks the option whose label begins with the entry's name) |
| `RevertWildShape()` | `WildShapeRules.CanRevert`, then `GameManager.TryWildShapeRevertForAI` (added 2026-10-14, CHR-020) | the Wild Shape button in animal form (it changes back) |
| `BardicMusic(ability, targets...)`, `InspireCompetence(ally, skill)` | `BardicMusicRules.CanStart` (refused with the rule's reason: no bardic music, Perform ranks, no use left, no standard action, grappling, silenced), then `GameManager.TryBardicMusicForAI` (added 2026-10-15, CHR-026) | the Bardic Music button, the song's option, then each target's option (Fascinate; Begin Fascinate when fewer than the bard's count were named), or the ally's and the skill's option (Inspire Competence) |
| `BardicConcentrate()`, `BardicSuggestion(t)`, `BardicStop()` | `BardicMusicRules.CanConcentrate` / `SuggestionTargetProblem` / a performance under way, then `TryBardicConcentrateForAI`, `TryBardicSuggestionForAI`, `TryBardicStopForAI` | the Bardic Music button, then the Concentrate, Suggestion or Stop option |
| `NaturalAttack(t, name)` | not allowed (Ui only) | one natural-attack button press for the natural attack `name` (the first unused one of that name, or a used one while Haste's extra natural attack is unused, as `ActionButtonPanel.BuildNaturalAttackButtonOptions` offers it; `OnNaturalAttackButtonPressed`) and the target square |
| `FullAttack(t, hasteAttack)` | as `Attack`, refused without the full round; `hasteAttack` is a build error (the AI picks with `NaturalAttackChoice`) | Full Attack button, then the target. While the step is in flight the coroutine's optional 5-foot step prompts are skipped (a click on the actor's own square, as a player skips; noted once), and Haste's natural-attack chooser (`GameManager.HasteNaturalAttackMenuName`, CMB-124) is answered with the option whose label starts with `hasteAttack (`, or Cancel (the default natural attack) when `hasteAttack` is null or not offered; the answer is noted on the step |
| `Maneuver(type, t, offHand)` | `TryNPCSpecialAttackByTypeForAI`, then one frame; `BullRushCharge` is not allowed (use `Charge(t, bullRush: true)`) | `Harness_OpenSpecialAttackMenu`, then a click on that type's menu button (`Harness_PressSpecialAttackButton`; refused when a player could not click it), then the target square |
| `GrappleAction(kind)`, `GrappleAttack(name)` | not allowed | the grapple button: `Pin`, `ReleasePin` (also a holder's "Let go"), `Damage`, `Escape`, `Move`, or `Attack` (the Attack Unarmed or Natural Attack button). `GrappleAction(kind, prefix)` also answers the hold damage-mode menu that `Pin`, `Escape` and `Move` open for an Improved Grab grabber whose won check deals hold damage (MM p.310; CMB-148), clicking the option starting with `prefix` (`Lethal damage` or `Nonlethal damage`); a plain `GrappleAction` answers it `Lethal damage`. `GrappleAttack(name)` presses `Attack` and, when the natural-attack menu opens (CMB-127), clicks the first option whose label starts with `name (` (`Rake (` for the rake, CMB-144): the unused attack of that name, else its Haste option (`CombatUI.Harness_FindSpecialStyleOption`); with one option left and no rake the game makes it at once and the step notes that no menu opened |
| `GrappleDamage(prefix)` | not allowed | the Grapple: Damage button (a holder's Damage check too, CMB-163), then the damage-mode menu option whose label starts with `prefix` (default `Lethal damage`) |
| `GrappleMove(x, y)`, `GrappleEscape(x, y)` | not allowed | the Grapple: Move or Escape Check button, then, when the check is won and the destination selection opens, the square (x, y): the drag's destination or the escaper's adjacent square (CMB-152); a failed check notes that no selection opened |
| `AnswerImprovedGrab(choice)` | skipped | answers the PC's Improved Grab menu that the step before it opened (CMB-148): `grapple`, `hold` or `skip`; with no such step the menu is answered Skip and noted on the step in flight |
| `Cast(spellId, t, metamagic)` | `TryNPCPerformSpellCastForAI` with `SpellDatabase.GetSpell(id).Clone()` (never the template) and the step's metamagic (since batch 11 step 21; the NPC path applies and spends it as the PC menu does); an area-routed spell (`AreaAimSearch.IsAreaCastForAI`, Prayer included) goes through `TryNPCPerformAreaSpellCastForAI` at the aim search's best aim whose area holds `t` (since the batch 11 step 25 review) | Cast button, the first unused prepared slot with that spell (for a spontaneous caster the spell from `GetCastablePreparedSpells`, the menu's list, since 2026-10-14) through `Harness_SelectSpell` (a melee touch spell's Cast Now / Discharge Later prompt is answered Cast Now through `Harness_AnswerTouchSpellPromptCastNow` and noted on the step; `Harness_PendingPrompt` reports it as `touch-spell`), then the target; a self-centered burst (Bless, Prayer) opens a preview instead, which the step confirms through `Harness_ConfirmSelfAoE` (the player's left click; added 2026-10-09) |
| `Charge(t, bullRush, hasteAttack)` | `CanChargeTargetForAI`, then `NPCExecuteChargeForAI`; `hasteAttack` is a build error | Charge button (or the menu's Bull Rush (Charge) button), target, confirmation; a pounce's Haste chooser is answered as for `FullAttack` |
| `CastChoosing(spellId, t, choices...)` | not allowed (Ui only; the NPC path's choices are the AI decider's, `AiCastDecider`, since batch 11 step 22) | as `Cast`, answering each selection list the cast opens with the next choice: an option label prefix (`Harness_ChooseSelectionOption`) or `*` for the first option (`Harness_ChooseFirstSelectionOption`), before the target click (the Resist Energy type) and after it (the Magic Weapon weapon); an unanswered choice is noted on the step (added 2026-10-15, batch 11 step 3) |
| `CastAt(spellId, x, y, choices...)` | not allowed (Ui only; the NPC path casts at an actor) | as `CastChoosing`, but the click goes to the square (x, y): a Summon Monster or Summon Swarm destination (the creature list of `ShowSummonCreatureSelection` answers `*` or a label prefix too, through `CombatUI.Harness_FindSummonCreatureOption`) or a Grease area's anchor (added 2026-10-15, batch 11 step 10) |
| `CastHeld(spellId)`, `DeliverHeld(t)` | not allowed (Ui only) | `CastHeld`: the Cast button and the prepared spell, then Discharge Later on the touch-spell prompt (`Harness_AnswerTouchSpellPromptDischargeLater`; the caster holds the charge through the cast core, `BeginHoldTouchCharge`). `DeliverHeld`: the Discharge button (`OnDischargeHeldTouchButtonPressed`), then the target (added 2026-10-15) |
| `Convert(sacrificedSpellId, level, t, metamagic)` | not allowed (Ui only) | A cleric's spontaneous conversion: the Cast button, then the spell list's Convert button on the prepared spell of that level and the metamagic panel's choice (`Harness_SelectSpontaneousConversion`, which calls `CombatUI.BeginSpontaneousConversion` as the button does), then the cure or inflict spell's target as `Cast` (touch prompt answered Cast Now). With metamagic the cast core gives up a convertible prepared spell of the adjusted level (added 2026-10-16, batch 11 step 21) |
| `UseItem(itemId, t)` | not allowed (Ui only) | `TryUseConsumableFromInventory` with the first pack item of that ItemDatabase id (the Use Item panel's call), so a scroll or wand spell enters the cast pipeline (`InitiateScrollCastThroughPipeline`, `InitiateWandCastThroughPipeline`); the target's square is clicked as soon as the target selection opens, after the AoO confirmations the item use raises (answer them with `AnswerAoO` steps); a selection that never opens is noted (added 2026-10-15) |
| `AnswerAoO(Proceed/Defensive/Cancel)` | skipped | `Harness_AnswerAoO` while the confirmation is open; an answer not offered is refused and the confirmation answered Cancel (never Proceed) |
| `Pass()`, `EndTurn()`, `Assert(name, check)`, `Wait(frames)` | nothing; end the turn (later steps dropped); an `assert` event with the result (`Expect.AssertsPass`); wait | the same (End Turn button) |

On the AI path a grappling actor's movement and ordinary attack steps are refused: a script bypasses the grapple turn rules (`RunGrappleTurnForAI`), so leave such turns to the AI. The game also gives a grappling creature no ordinary movement (`GetCurrentMoveRangeSquares` is 0, PHB p.156), whoever controls it. The AI-path executors are yielded as nested coroutines, so `SafeEnumerate` catches their exceptions. On the Ui path a step acts only on a fresh frame when the actor is the active PC, the sub-phase is ChoosingAction and `Harness_PendingPrompt` is null. A click that chose nothing (an invalid square) is cancelled back to the action menu and the step ends refused. An AoO confirmation with no `AnswerAoO` step next is answered Proceed and noted on the step in flight, or traced as its own auto `AnswerAoO` step when none is in flight. A step settles when the action menu returns, the turn passes or combat ends; one that does not settle, or a menu that does not come back, within 600 frames AND 30 game-seconds (both, as in the core watchdog; fast mode spends about one game-second per frame, while watch mode runs hundreds of frames per real second) ends the job as SoftLock with the diagnosis. When the game ends a Ui turn inside a step and the next turn is another Ui actor's, the runner abandons the old driver first (its in-flight step is traced, the rest dropped). The Improved Grab menu (`ImprovedGrabMenu`) is answered from an `AnswerImprovedGrab` step, else Skip, the grapple damage-mode menu by the `GrappleDamage` step, and the hold damage-mode menu (`GrappleHoldDamageModeMenu`, opened by Pin, Escape, Move and Use Opponent's Weapon while Improved Grab hold damage applies) by `GrappleAction(kind, prefix)`, or `Lethal damage` for a plain `GrappleAction`. Other prompts are not answered and end a Ui job that way: the ranged retarget, the full-attack 5-ft choice and other special-style submenus (`Harness_PendingPrompt` reports these three as `ranged-retarget`, `full-attack-5ft` and `submenu`), and bull rush push and follow, the disarm item choice and the dual-wield prompt. The rules scenarios strip the Fighter kit's shield and spiked gauntlet from Ui fighters so no dual-wield prompt opens. Not exercised by any catalog scenario yet: on the Ui path `Cast`, `DropProne`, `StandUp`, `FullAttack`, and `Escape` without a square; on the AI path `StandUp` (`rules/prone-ai-stands` uses the AI, not a step); `Assert` and `Wait` on either path, and `EndTurn` on the AI path. Expectation helpers for steps: `TraceView.Steps`, `TraceView.LogInts` (integers parsed from combat log lines), `Expect.StepStatus`, `Expect.AssertsPass`, `Expect.Controller`.

**Job phases** (per scenario, seed and repetition; `Start` only schedules the run, and the first job begins on the next frame on the runner's own coroutine): preflight (Play mode, a ready GameManager, the project path; refused with `needsFresh` after static suites in the same Play session or after a dirty job), boot once per Play session (`CharacterCreationUI.CompleteWithParty` with the Quick Start party, the real creation path; default Fighter, Rogue, Cleric, Wizard), reset and clean check (`Harness_ResetWorld`, then no active pool slot, every `NPCs` entry a pool slot (`CharacterController.IsEncounterPoolSlot`; before ENC-001 the check compared the list's length with the first job's, which a grown pool would break), no extra live controller, no area effect, the GameEventSystem subscriber counts of the first job, no hook left set; a failure ends the job as Contaminated and stops the run), seed (`Random.InitState(seed)`, HP mode Average), spawn and place, control, hooks (trace, invariants, dice filter, input suppression, log listener, fast mode), start (`Random.InitState(seed * 7919 + 17)`, `Harness_StartCombat`), monitor (every frame), halt (twice, a frame apart, CORE-003), finalize, cleanup (hooks cleared, world reset, party rest, and control, profiles, RNG, HP mode and fast mode restored; each step guarded on its own). The whole party is rebuilt every job, so repetitions start from the same state. An exception that escapes any phase ends that job as Exception with a result, runs its cleanup, marks the session dirty and stops the run, so status.json always reaches a final state. Only profiles the harness created are destroyed; an `ActorSpec.Profile` override should return a fresh `ScriptableObject.CreateInstance` (an asset, or an instance another controller uses, is assigned but never destroyed).

**Running it through the Unity MCP.** Never edit scripts or call `AssetDatabase.Refresh` while a run is going. Depending on Preferences > General > Script Changes While Playing (not checked on this machine), a recompile either stops Play mode or reloads the scripts in place; either way the run is lost, and the runner writes status.json with `"state":"interrupted"` and a summary with run verdict INTERRUPTED. Exit Play mode and start a fresh session. Do not run the static suites during a run either: `StaticSuiteRunner` refuses while a run is in progress, and a pass that slips through ends the job as Contaminated.

1. `bash tools/compile_check.sh`; in edit mode `AssetDatabase.Refresh()` so Unity compiles the change.
2. `EditorApplication.EnterPlaymode();` (a fresh Play session; never one that ran static suites).
3. In a later command: `Tests.Scenarios.ScenarioHarness.Start("smoke/*", "1-3", "repeat=2")`. Filter: comma-separated id globs. Seeds: `1`, `1-5`, `1,3,7`. Options (`key=value;...`): `mode=fast|watch`, `maxRounds=N`, `repeat=N` (compares the trace hash of each repetition per seed), `sweep=N` (seeds 1..N, aggregated per id), `record=dice`, `keep` (stop after the first job and leave the world for a human), `wallCap=seconds` (default the definition's `.WallCap(seconds)`, else 120). `ScenarioHarness.Start(def, seeds, options)` runs a definition built inline in the CommandScript with `Scenario.Define(...)...Build()`, with no new file and no recompile. `ScenarioHarness.List(filter)` lists the catalog. Start returns `runId`, `jobs`, `statusPath` and `summaryPath` (absolute paths), and `loadErrors` when a catalog source threw (its remaining scenarios are missing from the run); it refuses with `needsFresh: true` in a dirty session.
4. Wait without the MCP, with a bounded loop run through Monitor or a background Bash (a foreground `sleep` is blocked in the agent environment): `for i in $(seq 1 200); do grep -qE '"state":"(done|interrupted)"' <statusPath> && break; sleep 3; done`. status.json is rewritten at most once a second while the run makes frames, at every job boundary and at the end; every write carries `heartbeatUtc` and `frame`. A heartbeat older than about 15 s while the state is still `running` means a dead run (a crash or a stopped coroutine): exit and re-enter Play mode. Or call `ScenarioHarness.Poll()` (never waits; it also unpauses the editor). `ScenarioHarness.Abort(reason)` ends the current job as Halted after its cleanup and skips the rest.
5. Read `Logs/Scenarios/<runId>/summary.json` (or `ScenarioHarness.Result()`), then grep the JSONL files.
6. `EditorApplication.ExitPlaymode();`. `ScenarioSessionGuard` clears any leftover hooks and fast mode.

**Files written** (`Logs/` is git-ignored): `Logs/Scenarios/<runId>/<id-slug>__s<seed>[__r<rep>].jsonl` (one event per line; its file name without `.jsonl` is the job id), `Logs/Scenarios/<runId>/<jobId>/caststages.jsonl` for a job that marked a cast stage (one stage per line, outside the hashed trace; section 3.5), `summary.json` (run verdict, totals, per-id aggregate, per-job results with violations, expectation lines and notes, waiver hits and `unusedWaivers`, frames, frames per second and editor-focus counts; `notRun` with a `resume` filter and seeds when the run stopped early; `loadErrors`; `warnings`, run-level warnings such as a combat-log leak the sweep had to clean up, section 3.5), `status.json` (state, `heartbeatUtc`, frame, current job and actor, totals; `summaryError` if the summary could not be written), all written through a temp file and `File.Replace` with retries, and one line per run in `Logs/Scenarios/index.jsonl` (run id, verdict, filter, seeds, options, totals, and `warnings`, the count of the summary's warnings, when there are any).

**Trace events.** Every line has `seq`, `f` (frames since the job started), `r` (round), `t` (turn index) and `ev`: `meta`; `actor` (key, team, control, source, class, level, HP, AC, touch AC, BAB, Hit Dice `hd`, racial Hit Dice `racialHd`, base saves `baseFort`, `baseRef` and `baseWill`, position, size, speed, AI profile, weapon); `init` (order with roll, modifier, total); `round`; `turn_start` (actor, controller `ai`, `scripted`, `idle` or `ui`, and a snapshot of every actor: HP, nonlethal, position, sorted conditions, HP state, grappling, team); `turn_end` (action-economy flags and the snapshots that changed); `move` (from, to, movement type, squares); `attack` (die, total, modifier = total - die, AC, hit, damage, natural 20 and 1, threat, confirmation, AoO flag and trigger, flanking and its bonus, sneak attack, ranged, weapon, weapon damage dice `dice` and their roll `baseRoll` (0 on a miss; a confirmed critical's roll includes the extra dice), the static damage modifier `dmgMod` and its terms `dmgTerms` ("label:value" joined by "; ", from `CombatResult.WeaponDamageBonus`, CMB-003), the weapon damage before riders `weaponDmg` (0 on a miss), the number of times the weapon damage was rolled `dmgRolls` (the critical multiplier on a confirmed critical, else 1; 0 on a miss; CMB-004), the sneak attack damage `sneakDmg`, `critImmune` (a threat against a creature immune to critical hits: the damage is not multiplied; CMB-064), `critEffects` (that threat was confirmed, so only the weapon's critical-hit effects apply, DMG p.222), concealment miss); `aoo` (trigger: movement, maneuver, spellcast, standup, bullrush-move, ranged, item, pickup or other); `maneuver`; `threatened_cast`; `cast` (a cast begins: caster, target, spell id and level; raised by the NPC path and, since batch 11 step 3 (2026-10-15), by the PC pipelines at their cost point, `PerformSpellCast`, `PerformAoESpellCast`, `HoldPendingMeleeTouchCharge` and `TryConsumePendingSpellCast`, for scroll and wand casts too; the target is null for an area, summon, Grease or held-charge cast, and the delivery of a held charge is not a cast); `generated` (what a definition's `GenerateActors` produced, for example the DMG encounter); `cond`; `hp`; `nl`; `dice` (with `record=dice`, or whenever a force fired); `log` (combat log text without rich-text tags); `phase` (with `by`, the GameManager methods that set CombatOver); `step`; `note`; `decision`; `violation`; `watchdog`; `log-leak` (the combat-log leak sweep found a leak: root lines destroyed, when, and the panel's `holders` and `lines` when they were out of bounds, section 3.5); `end` (outcome and final snapshots); `expectation` (`known` names the issue of a known-behaviour pin; the field is set only on pins); `verdict`. The trace hash is SHA-1 over every line except `watchdog` and `log-leak` events (both depend on frame timing), without the fields `f`, `rep`, `utc`, `gitHead`, `wallSec`, `mode` (fast or watch, so the two modes can be compared) and `diag` (the timing-dependent detail of a SoftLock or Timeout decision). The `meta` event's `harness` field is 6 since the attack event gained `dmgRolls`, `sneakDmg`, `critImmune` and `critEffects` (2026-10-09, CMB-004; 5 from `dmgMod`, `dmgTerms` and `weaponDmg`, 2026-10-09, CMB-003; 4 from the actor event's `hd`, `racialHd` and the base saves, 2026-10-08, CRE-004; 3 from the attack event's `dice` and `baseRoll`, 2 from the `cast` event); compare hashes only between traces of the same harness version and code.

**Invariants** (a broken one is a `violation` event): `hp-bounds` (HP at most max plus temporary HP; HP state consistent with HP), `grid` (on the grid, in its square's occupancy, no shared square unless grappling, the two are still in the square a pin release left them in (the release ends the grapple and moves neither, PHB p.157, CMB-089, CMB-122; the allowance is granted only when a Pinned condition ends and neither creature grapples afterwards, so a grapple ended by an escape, which must move the escaper, gets none; it ends when either leaves that square), one of the two is helpless (PHB p.148) or one is a swarm (MM p.237 and p.316); grapple opponents at most 1 square apart, or their natural reach if larger, since a grappling creature takes no ordinary movement and the grapple's own move drags its opponents along, PHB p.156-157), `turn-structure` (turn start and end pair up, one turn per actor per round, rounds never go back, no turn for a dead actor), `no-act-when-down` (no attack or maneuver by a dead, dying, stable or unconscious actor; a disabled actor, at 0 HP or at negative HP with Diehard, may act. The threatened-cast hook fires after the AoOs the cast provoked, so a caster dropped by one of them correctly loses the spell; only a spell that still goes off with the caster down is flagged), `attack-math` (a natural 1 never hits, the flanking bonus is 0 or +2, hit agrees with total against AC unless a natural 20, concealment, a protection barrier or Deflect Arrows, which the trace recognises by its "deflected" note), `econ` (no full-round action when the turn started disabled or staggered, no 5-ft step plus other own movement, at most 4x speed; moves are reported per segment, so the squares are a lower bound. The bull rush follow and overrun moves arrive as ordinary moves, so the squares moved since the actor's last attack, AoO or maneuver are taken back when a bull rush or overrun event arrives; whether a follow after a 5-ft step breaks the step is not settled RAW and is not flagged), `aoo-budget` (AoOs since the threatener's last turn at most 1, plus the DEX modifier with `Combat Reflexes`), `prone-move` (no own move of more than 1 square while prone, CMB-074), `team-stable` (no team change without Charmed or Commanded), `no-errors` (a LogError or LogAssert from game code is a violation; an exception from game code ends the job as Exception, halted by the runner on the next frame because halting inside a log callback is unsafe. Origin comes from the stack trace: a frame under `Assets/Scripts/Tests/Scenarios/` or in `ScenarioHooks.cs` is the harness, already reported as `hook-error`; no `Assets/Scripts/` frame at all, as for editor windows or an agent's CommandScript, is foreign; both only add a job note), `combat-end-detected`, `combat-end-early`, `phase-after-combatover`, `cast-stack` (the per-cast context stack was misused: `ScenarioHooks.CastStackViolation`, raised by `GameManager.BeginCast`/`EndCast` when a cast ends while another is on top, a Ui cast begins inside an AI cast or a Ui exit tries to end an AI cast; batch 11 step 4), and `hook-error` (a harness handler threw; always an Error).

**Combat end.** The harness uses the game's own predicate (owner definition 2026-10-07): `CombatEndRules.IsOutOfFight` (`Combat/Core/CombatEndRules.cs`), read through `ScenarioChecks.IsOutOfFight`, `SideState` (which reads `GameManager.GetCombatEndSides`, the counts `EvaluateCombatEnd` uses) and the snapshots' `out` flag (`ScenarioChecks.IsDownSnapshot`). A creature is out of the fight when it is dead, dying or unconscious, with or without regeneration or fast healing. Unconscious covers stable, knocked out by nonlethal damage, the Unconscious condition, asleep (the Asleep condition alone, as a sleep arrow applies it, counts) and petrified (considered unconscious: PHB p.311, DMG p.301). A disabled creature stays in, at 0 HP (PHB p.145) or at negative HP with Diehard (PHB p.93; healing at negative HP keeps a Diehard creature disabled, not stable, since 2026-10-08). A side is out when every active member of that team (party, pool and summons) is out and it has an active member or had one earlier in the combat (a side whose last creature was removed is out). Because the harness reads the game's own predicate, `combat-end-detected` and `combat-end-early` catch a missing check, not a wrong predicate: `Tests.Combat.CombatEndRulesTests` pins the predicate case by case from the owner definition. When the game sets CombatOver, the outcome is Victory or Defeat from the side states (if neither side is out, the game ended combat early: `combat-end-early`). When a side is out and the game has not ended combat by the end of that turn, the harness records `combat-end-detected`, sets VictoryUndetected or DefeatUndetected and halts the fight itself. Since the game checks at every turn boundary (`NextInitiativeTurn`), both are now unwaived failures; the `cause=hp<=0` / `cause=unconscious>0` split of the detail went with CORE-011 and CORE-037 (fixed 2026-10-08). Owner interpretations 2026-10-11 (CORE-038): a hostile creature charmed by or under the control of the party side counts as out of the hostile side (`CombatEndRules.IsControlledByTeam`, in `GetCombatEndSides`, so the harness sees it too); when both sides are out at once the game rolls the dying PCs' stabilization out and declares the outcome, and the harness takes Victory or Defeat from the handler that set CombatOver (the nearest `HandleCombatVictoryDetected` or `HandleCombatDefeatDetected` frame in the phase event's `by`, `ScenarioChecks.DeclaredOutcome`; a caller name such as `CheckCombatVictory` does not count) instead of calling every tie a Defeat.

**Watchdog.** SoftLock after 300 frames and 60 game-seconds without a new event (repeated identical log lines do not count); SkipChainRunaway when more than 3x the initiative count turns start in one frame (CORE-012; halted inside the turn-start handler); Stalemate when a round past MaxRounds begins; Timeout at the wall-clock cap; Paused on a second editor pause that no logged error just preceded (a human or debugger pause). A pause in the frame of a logged error, or the next one, is Console Error Pause: the runner unpauses it at once and only notes it, so waived errors stay usable whatever the Console toggle says. SoftLock and Timeout add a diagnosis to the job notes and the decision's `diag`: `Harness_DumpState`, the active Canvas objects whose names contain Prompt, Dialog, Confirm or Panel, and the last 20 trace lines. Contaminated mid-job when a static-suite pass ran or the hooks were cleared. A soft lock, a runaway, an exception, a contamination or a dirty reset marks the session dirty: later Starts answer `needsFresh` until a new Play session.

**Verdicts.** Error (SetupFailed, SoftLock, SkipChainRunaway, Exception, Paused, Timeout, Contaminated, or a hook error), Fail (an expectation without an XFail issue failed, or an unwaived violation), XPass (an expectation marked `ExpectXFail(issue, ...)` passed, so the issue may be fixed; reported loudly), XFail (only XFail expectations failed), Inconclusive (an expectation could not decide, or the job was aborted: outcome Halted), Known (only waived violations, or a known-behaviour pin that held: `ExpectKnown(issue, name, check)`, added 2026-10-15 for batch 11, states today's behaviour that the filed `issue` says is not the rule or not yet shown to be; a pin is not a ruling, and when it fails the job is Fail: the fix that changes the behaviour rewrites the pin as a plain expectation of the rule, anything else is a regression), Pass. A sweep aggregates per id, first match wins: Error, Fail or XPass if any seed had it; Pass if any seed passed; Known if any seed was Known; Inconclusive if every seed was; else XFail. Run verdict: INTERRUPTED (Play mode ended or scripts reloaded mid-run), ERROR (also when jobs were left without a result), LOADERROR (a catalog definition or source failed to load, so the run may be a subset; see `loadErrors`), FAIL, XPASS, NONDETERMINISTIC (a repetition's hash differed; the job's `firstDiffSeq` is the first differing line), ABORTED or OK. An OK run in which some id aggregated to Inconclusive (every seed undecided, so the run proved nothing for it) reads `OK (n inconclusive)`, and status.json and summary.json list those ids in `inconclusiveIds`: check it before trusting an OK, and widen the seed range for those ids. Expected Fail jobs: none since 2026-10-14 (TST-038 fixed with CMB-028, TST-039 and TST-040 with CRE-062); a full `rules/*` run is OK, with XFail only for the issues its expectations name. Any Fail is a regression.

**Waivers.** `KnownIssueWaivers.Global`: CORE-003 for `phase-after-combatover` (the CORE-011 and CORE-037 combat-end waivers were deleted when those issues were fixed on 2026-10-08). A definition adds its own with `.Waive(issue, invariant, detailRegex)`. Every run lists the waivers that never matched in `unusedWaivers`; delete a waiver that stays unused after its issue is fixed. Add an error-log waiver only after seeing the real message, always with its issue ID.

**Writing a scenario.** Put it in `Catalog/<Area>Scenarios.cs`; prefer typed steps over coroutine scripts; name each expectation after the rule it checks (for example "Flanking gives +2 and leaves AC unchanged (PHB p.153, CMB-001)"); pin branch-critical dice by context with `.Force(sides, value, ctxSubstring, count)` (DiceService contexts such as "Trip touch attack"; context-free `DiceRoller` dice can be forced only by sides, and the raw `Random.Range` sites of TST-033 not at all); keep expectations structural, since any added or removed `Random` call shifts every later roll. Confirm NPC ids with `NPCDatabase.Get` first. Dice contexts in use (grep `DiceService.D20("` for the rest): "Trip touch attack", "Trip Strength check", "Trip defense check", "Touch attack" (the grapple touch attack), "Grapple check" (every grapple check, both sides; ties go to the higher modifier), "Grapple opposed check (attacker)" and "(defender)" (moving the grapple), "Bull rush check", "Bull rush defense", "Disarm attack roll", "Disarm defense roll", "Sunder attack roll", "Sunder defense roll", "Overrun attack roll", "Overrun defense roll", "Counter-trip check" (the defender's check when it trips back) and "Counter-trip resist check" (the original tripper's). Weapon attack rolls, initiative and the Concentration check (`ConcentrationManager`, `DiceRoller.D20`) have no context. Where a branch rests on such a roll, write the expectation for every branch (for example: a damaging AoO foils the disarm, otherwise the target is disarmed), return `ExpectResult.Inconclusive` when the branch under test did not come up, and run a seed range: a sweep aggregates to Pass when any seed passed and none failed.

**Limits of AI-run party members.** They take the NPC turn path: its own turn-start order (CMB-075), no rage ticks (CHR-069; bardic music ticks at the initiative count for both paths since 2026-10-15), no walls, Summon Swarm, two-weapon fighting or two metamagic feats on one cast (AI-071, AI-055, AI-053, AI-072; area spells since batch 11 step 25, summons, Dimension Door and held charges since step 27, the AI's own metamagic choice since step 28; the AI flurries since 2026-10-14, CHR-003); see [systems/PC_NPC_PARITY.md](systems/PC_NPC_PARITY.md) section 2. The `controller` field of every turn says which path ran. PC executors are covered only by Ui actors. Prompts other than the AoO confirmation and the counter-trip prompt (the ranged retarget, the full-attack 5-ft choice, a special-style submenu, bull rush push and follow, Improved Grab, the disarm item choice, the dual-wield prompt; the touch-spell prompt is answered Cast Now by the Ui `Cast` step) are not answered yet and end a Ui job as a SoftLock (see Scripted steps). The counter-trip prompt (PHB p.158, CMB-079: a controllable defender after a trip against it failed at the opposed check) can open on any turn, so the runner answers it every frame, before the Ui driver (`HandleCounterTripPrompt`, through `GameManager.Harness_AnswerCounterTrip`, the same answer as the buttons): Trip Back, or Decline for an actor named in `.CounterTripAnswer(key, false)`; each answer is a `note` event ("counter-trip prompt for <key> answered TripBack|Decline"), and `Harness_PendingPrompt` reports `counter-trip` while it is open. An AI-path `Maneuver` step settles only after the answer, as the NPC coroutines wait for it.

**Rules scenarios** (`Catalog/RulesScenarios.cs`, tag `rules`; NPC data and the PHB pages are in the file header). Stats actors are Human fighters (STR 16) with BAB set through `BaseAttackBonusOverride`.

| Scenario | Checks |
|---|---|
| `rules/movement-aoo-move`, `-5ft`, `-withdraw` | an orc leaving the fighter's reach provokes exactly one movement AoO before it leaves the square; a 5-ft step and a withdraw provoke none (CMB-005, CMB-073; PHB p.137, p.143-144) |
| `rules/movement-aoo-withdraw-far`, `-ui` | a withdraw of 9 squares, (10,10) to (19,10), away from the only enemy, at (9,10): beyond one move (a fixture step checks the mover's budget: 30 ft for the hero, 40 ft for the barbarian orc) and within the double move, ends there and provokes no AoO (PHB p.143). Scripted: an NPC orc through the NPC withdraw executor; `-ui`: a PC hero through the Withdraw button and click, which before GRID-001 (2026-10-14) was cut to one move and provoked on leaving the start square |
| `rules/movement-aoo-withdraw-large`, `-ui` | a Large withdrawer based at (10,10) (its space (10,10)-(11,11)) withdraws 6 squares west to (4,10) from a visible enemy at (11,9) that threatens (10,10) and (11,10); the first step vacates (11,10), the second (10,10), and neither provokes, since the whole starting space is exempt for the whole withdraw (PHB p.143, DMG p.29; delegated RAW reading 2026-10-14, GRID-021; before the fix the second step provoked). A fixture step checks the size, the threatened squares, the enemy's AoO and that the mover sees it. Scripted: an NPC ogre through the NPC withdraw executor; `-ui`: a PC hero under Enlarge Person through the Withdraw button and click |
| `rules/movement-through-ally`, `-ui` | a move of 3 squares, (10,10) to (13,10), whose straight path crosses an ally at (11,10), with the only enemy far away: the move ends at (13,10), its segments are contiguous (each starts where the last ended, 3 squares in all; on the Ui path every step is 1 square), the ally stays and nothing provokes (PHB p.148). Scripted: an NPC orc past a goblin through the NPC move executor; `-ui`: a PC hero past a fighter through the Move button and click, whose per-step executor before 2026-10-14 checked every step as the destination, refused the ally's square and jumped two squares |
| `rules/movement-aoo-double-move`, `-ui` | a double move, (11,10) to (11,11) and on to (14,11): a Combat Reflexes threatener (DEX 16) of both start squares takes one movement AoO, in the first move only, and a guard that threatens only (11,11) takes one in the second move; 2 AoOs in all. Scripted: an NPC orc through the NPC move executor; `-ui`: a PC hero through the Move button and the AoO prompt (owner ruling 2026-10-08, CMB-128; PHB p.92, p.138) |
| `rules/flanking`, `rules/flanking-control` | Quick Start rogue and fighter: opposite allies flank with +2, the AC stays the setup AC, the rogue's hits add sneak attack; allies not opposite do not flank (CMB-001; PHB p.153) |
| `rules/prone-ai-stands` | a prone AI orc stands up and the fighter's standup AoO comes before Prone is removed (CMB-074) |
| `rules/prone-ui-crawl` | a prone Ui fighter: Move refused, Crawl 1 square after the orc's movement AoO, still prone with the move action spent (PHB p.143) |
| `rules/prone-scripted-move` | a prone scripted orc that does not stand cannot move (the hook sits before the AI's automatic stand-up) |
| `rules/npc-cast-threatened`, `-threatened-normal`, `-unthreatened` | the Magic Missile adept casting adjacent: a spellcast AoO before the spell unless cast defensively, none when defensive; none when unthreatened (SPL-006; PHB p.140). The AI adept always casts defensively (Concentration +5 against DC 16, 50%); `-normal` lowers its CON to 6 (+3, 40%, below `ShouldCastDefensively`'s 50%) and scripts the cast through the NPC cast executor, because left to the AI that adept retreats first |
| `rules/maneuver-trip`, `-trip-improved`, `-trip-fail`, `-trip-ui` | forced dice: AoO before the trip without Improved Trip, none with it; defender prone and no counter-trip after a trip that lands; the PC menu path gives the same result. `-trip-improved` (fighter 6 with Improved Trip, then an `Attack` step): the feat's attack comes right after the trip at the trip's bonus and the `Attack` step still makes the +1 iterative, so 2 attacks 5 apart (modifiers 9 and 4; PHB p.96). `-trip-fail`: after the lost opposed check the AI-run orc trips back (forced counter-trip dice), with no touch attack, AoO or action, and the fighter ends prone (PHB p.158, CMB-079) |
| `rules/maneuver-trip-counter-ui`, `-counter-ui-decline` | a scripted orc's trip against a Ui hero fails at the opposed check; the hero's counter-trip prompt is answered by the runner (Trip Back, or Decline through `.CounterTripAnswer`): the forced counter-trip lands and the orc ends prone before the orc's step settles, or nothing follows (PHB p.158, CMB-079) |
| `rules/maneuver-trip-counter-ui-ai`, `-counter-ui-ai-decline` | an AI-run orc (Humanoid profile, BAB raised to +6, no typed steps) trips a Ui hero who makes no AoOs; the trip fails at the opposed check and `PerformNPCMeleeAttackSequence` suspends while the hero's prompt is open; the runner answers (Trip Back or Decline): exactly one prompt, no orc attack while it is open, then the orc's remaining +1 iterative and the end of its turn, and the hero's turn after it; with Trip Back the forced counter-trip lands first and the orc ends prone (PHB p.158, CMB-079) |
| `rules/maneuver-trip-size` | a Small halfling fighter (`FighterOfRace`): its trip of a Large ogre is refused before any cost or AoO; its trip of a Medium orc is attempted (PHB p.158, CMB-079) |

| `rules/maneuver-disarm` | the defender's AoO first; a damaging AoO foils the disarm, otherwise the target is disarmed (PHB p.155) |

**Cast pipeline pins** (`Catalog/CastPipelineScenarios.cs`, tags `rules` and `cast-pipeline`; batch 11 step 3, 2026-10-15, [designs/cast_core_batch11.md](designs/cast_core_batch11.md)). They pin today's cast behaviour before the cast pipelines are merged: Assert steps record what happened as trace notes `cpp key=value` (facts), and the expectations judge the facts, a plain expectation for a rule that holds today and a known-behaviour pin (`ExpectKnown`, verdict Known) for today's behaviour that a filed issue says is not the rule; a pin is not a ruling, and the later step that changes the behaviour rewrites it. Casters are Stats-built Humans (Wizard INT 16, Cleric and Druid WIS 16, the class kit); the spells go into prepared slots as clones, and a wizard that needs a 3rd-, 4th- or 5th-level slot gets one added (the wizard table stops at 2nd-level slots, SPL-041). Targets are `target_dummy` (no weapon, so it threatens nothing) unless a rule needs another creature; saves are decided with a +-30 save tweak and the damage dice forced, so every seed gives the same facts. Every pair also checks that both casts ran (both steps `done`, one `cast` event per caster), so equal end states after two refused casts cannot pass. Verdicts on 2026-10-15 after the step 3 review (seeds 1-3, run 20261015-102844): 6 Pass, 17 Known.

|---|---|

A dummy raises a Lesser Globe of Invulnerability; the Ui wizard 7's plain Magic Missile is stopped (0), one heightened to 4th from an added 4th-level slot prepared heightened passes (four missiles at 5 = 20); the globe test of the stamped heightened clone is false |
| `rules/cast-ally-in-burning-hands` | Pass since batch 11 step 18 (SPL-149, PHB p.177). Burning Hands through an ally fighter and a dummy, both with +30 on saves: both roll their Reflex saves, make them and take 10 (before, the ally forgave its save and took 20) |
| `rules/sleep-spell-penetration` | Batch 11 step 18 (SPL-122; PHB p.177, p.100). A Ui wizard 1 with Spell Penetration casts Sleep on two goblins that fail every save; every "Sleep SR check" d20 is 10, so the check is 13: g1 (SR 13) sleeps, g2 (SR 14) does not, and the blocked line names the +2 Spell Penetration |
| `rules/spell-save-bonus-custom-handler` | Batch 11 step 18 (SPL-018; PHB p.266, p.231). A chaotic evil Ui wizard 5's Fireball on two dummies whose save tweak puts d20 10 + their Reflex modifier at the DC less 1: d2 takes 30, d1 under Protection from Evil gets the +2 ward on the handler's save and takes 15 |
| `rules/cast-entangled` | Pass (batch 11 step 13; Known for SPL-145 before). An entangled Ui wizard 5 casts Magic Missile with the check's d20 forced to 10 under its own label (`GameManager.EntangledConcentrationDiceContext`). The check is d20 + the casting Concentration bonus without Combat Casting (caster level + Con, CHR-085; PHB p.92; recorded as a fact, 6) against DC 15 + the spell's level, logged `d20 10 + conc 6 = 16 vs DC 16 (15 + spell level 1)`, and the missiles land (15) only because 16 reaches the DC (PHB p.308, p.70; delegated RAW reading 2026-10-14) |
| `rules/cast-silenced` | Pass (batch 11 step 13, SPL-092; PHB p.279, p.100). Three casters under a Silence (`Stats.SilenceActive`): the Ui wizard 5's Magic Missile is refused with one log line and nothing spent (by the spell menu before the cast begins, since the step 13 review; the core's Legality stage refuses it too), then its Silent Spell Magic Missile from a 2nd-level slot lands (15); a Scripted wizard's Magic Missile is refused (step status refused, no log line, nothing spent); a second Scripted wizard's prepared Silent Spell Magic Missile (a `MetamagicDataRef` record) lands (15); a fourth, Ui wizard holds a Shocking Grasp charge, is then silenced and picks Magic Missile: refused with one log line, the charge kept, nothing spent. Since batch 11 step 19 the prepared Silent Spell cast's result log names its metamagic (`SpellCaster.Cast` falls back to the metamagic on the spell clone), and the hand-built prepared spell carries `SpellData.IsPreparedMetamagicSlotSpell`, as the menu's does, so it counts its metamagic slot. Since batch 11 step 21 the Scripted cast carries that metamagic (`SpellCastRequest.ForAI`) and spends the Silent slot, and the Ui wizard's 2nd-level slot is prepared with Silent Spell (a metamagic cast spends only such a slot, SPL-012); trace hashes unchanged |
| `rules/cast-deafened-area` | Pass (batch 11 step 13, SPL-150; PHB p.307). A deafened Ui wizard 5's Burning Hands at two dummies that fail their saves, the deafened d% forced to 50: one `Deafened spell check` line for the whole cast and 20 damage to each; a deafened Scripted wizard's Magic Missile rolls once too and lands (15) |
| `rules/stale-cast-state-energy` | Batch 11 step 4 (SPL-112 stale-choice half, PHB p.272). A threatened Ui wizard picks cold for Resist Energy on itself and cancels at the AoO confirmation (nothing spent, no cast left open); a Scripted wizard's Resist Energy then resists fire, its own request's choice (`AiCastDecider`: the goblin has no energy attack), not the cancelled cast's cold |
| `rules/stale-cast-state-fire-shield` | Batch 11 step 4 (PHB p.230). The Ui wizard's chill pick survives the Fire Shield prompt's re-entry of the target selection and reaches its own cast; the Scripted wizard's Fire Shield is warm (its request) |
| `rules/stale-cast-state-wand-asf` | Batch 11 step 4 (SPL-116 stale-flag part, PHB p.122). A Ui wizard uses its wand of magic missile and cancels at the target selection (`Harness_CancelSelection`; no charge spent, no cast left open); a Scripted wizard with 100% arcane spell failure then rolls it and loses its Magic Missile |
| `rules/stale-cast-state-f12-bypass` | Batch 11 step 4 (SPL-036). A Ui wizard with 100% arcane spell failure clicks the F12 panel twice (the second cast replaces the first, still at its target selection, and keeps the panel's caster), then casts Magic Missile through `TestCastSpellFromPanel`: no failure check is rolled, the missiles deal 15, and the cast ends with the panel's caster forgotten |
| `rules/cast-slot-failure-refund` | Pass (batch 11 step 14; Known for SPL-147 before). A Ui wizard 1 with Still Spell picks a stilled Magic Missile, which needs a 2nd-level slot it lacks (the harness selects it past the menu's slot filter): no spell, the slot kept, and every action left (the failed spend restores the snapshot; PHB p.141, p.143) |
| `rules/metamagic-cast-paths` | Pass (batch 11 step 21; SPL-055, SPL-040, SPL-148, SPL-012; PHB p.88, p.93, p.94, p.98). Every d4 and d8 at 4. A Ui sorcerer 6 (Empower Spell, Quicken Spell): a quickened Magic Missile is refused at the spell menu with one log line and nothing spent (PHB p.98 Special), an empowered one is a full-round action that spends a 3rd-level spell per day and deals 3 x 7 = 21. A Ui wizard 5 with a plain 1st-level and a Silent 2nd-level Magic Missile casts the plain one: the 1st-level slot is spent, a standard action, 15. A Scripted sorcerer 6's step Empower goes with the NPC cast: full round, a 3rd-level spell per day, 21. A Ui cleric 5 (Empower, Extend and Quicken Spell, spontaneous cure) converts its Bless on itself (`Convert` step): round 1 a quickened conversion is refused (one log line, nothing spent), an extended one gives up a 2nd-level spell, takes the full round and heals an unchanged 9 (Extend does not affect an instantaneous spell, PHB p.94; delegated RAW reading 2026-10-14); round 2 an empowered one gives up a 3rd-level spell, not the Bless, as a full-round action and heals 13 |
| `rules/npc-cast-prepared-metamagic` | Pass (batch 11 step 21; AI-001's metamagic-execution half, SPL-012; PHB p.88, p.93). Every d4 at 4. An AI-run wizard 5 (Empower Spell) whose only unspent slot is an added 3rd-level Empowered Magic Missile casts it on its own choice: 3 x 7 = 21, the slot spent. A Scripted wizard 5 with the same slot is refused a plain Magic Missile (no plain slot; nothing spent, every action left) and casts it with the step's Empower: 21, the slot spent |

**Runs of 2026-10-07** (Unity 6000.4.0f1 through the MCP, editor focused):
- Fresh Play session, `Start("smoke/*", "1-3", "repeat=2")`: 18 jobs, 18 Pass, run verdict OK, 14.7 s. smoke/boot ends Stalemate after one idle round with all six actors traced at their squares; smoke/two-goblins-ai and smoke/soak-mini end in a Victory the game detected (for example by `HandleCombatVictoryDetected < CheckCombatVictory < NPCExecuteCharge`). Every repetition had the same trace hash as the first, and the hashes for each seed matched those of an earlier Play session.
